## 生成 LRS 資料產品(遠程參考工具) · 使用率

**id**　eca4a35bcd33703f44-2

**english**　Generate LRS Data Product

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 資料產品工具

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-lrs-data-product.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-lrs-data-product.html

**text**　模板必須是一個 .json 文件。 在輸入路由特性參數中使用網絡特征分類 。 此工具不會修改輸入, 并且將創建 . csv 文件或地理資料庫表格作為輸出。 此工具支持選擇和定義查詢。 有效日期參數用于定義網絡的時間視圖。 只有指定日期上運行的路徑才能用來計算輸出。 對于長度和特性計數資料產品, 您可以指定一個以上的有效日期參數值。 如果指定了多個有效日期, 將會計算每對有效日期之間的長度或特征計數的三角形或變化。 當創建長度資料產品時, 請考慮以下內容: 使用能夠指定摘要和長度的 LRS 資料模板 。欄位。摘要和長度欄位必須存在于與 LRS 網絡相同的地理資料庫中。在從網絡的測量單位轉換為另一個單位時,支持單位轉換。邊界特征和摘要欄位參數只有在.json 文件沒有提供摘要欄位時才有效。輸出將根據LRS 網絡、摘要層和長度層所選特點的交叉點創建。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The template must be a .json file.\nUse a network feature class for the Input Route Features parameter.\nThis tool does not modify the inputs and will create a .csv file or a geodatabase table as the output.\nThis tool supports selection and definition query.\nThe Effective Date parameter is used to define the temporal view of the network. Only routes active on the date specified will be used for calculating the output.\nFor the length and feature count data products, you can specify more than one Effective Date parameter value. If multiple effective dates are specified, the delta or change in length or feature count between each pair of effective dates will be calculated.\nWhen creating a length data product, consider the following: Use an LRS data template that specifies the summary and length fields. The summary and length fields must exist in the same geodatabase as the LRS Network. Unit conversion is supported when converting from the network's measure units to another unit. The Boundary Features and Summary Field parameters are only valid when no summary field is provided in the .json file. The output will be created based on the intersection of selected features from the LRS Network, summary layer(s), and length layer(s).", "section": "Usage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-lrs-data-product.html#usage

## 生成 LRS 資料產品(遠程參考工具) · 使用率

**id**　eca4a35bcd33703f44-3

**english**　Generate LRS Data Product

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 資料產品工具

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-lrs-data-product.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-lrs-data-product.html

**text**　創建路線日志資料產品時,請考慮以下內容: 使用一個指定日志、位置和引用欄位的 LRS 資料模板。對數層和欄位必須存在于與 LRS 網絡相同的地理資料庫中。位置層和欄位必須是多邊形層,并存在于與 LRS 網絡相同的地理資料庫中。引用層和欄位必須是 LRS 點事件特征類或 LRS 交叉特性類,并且存在于與 LRS 網絡相同的地理資料庫中。輸出將基于來自 LRS 網絡、 log 層、 位置層和引用層選定特征的交叉點來創建。在創建特性計數資料產品時,應考慮下列因素: 使用 LRS 資料指定計數、摘要和路線標識欄位的模板。計數、摘要和路線標識欄位必須存在于與 LRS 網絡相同的地理資料庫中。只有當.json 文件沒有提供摘要欄位時,邊界特征和摘要欄位參數才有效。輸出將根據來自 LRS 網絡、摘要層和特性計數層選定特征的交叉點創建。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "When creating a route log data product, consider the following: Use an LRS data template that specifies the log, location, and referent fields. The log layer and fields must exist in the same geodatabase as the LRS Network. The location layer and fields must be a polygon layer and exist in the same geodatabase as the LRS Network. The referent layer and fields must be an LRS point event feature class or an LRS intersection feature class and exist in the same geodatabase as the LRS Network. The output will be created based on the intersection of selected features from the LRS Network, log layer(s), location layer(s), and referent layer.\nWhen creating a feature count data product, consider the following: Use an LRS data template that specifies the count, summary, and route identifier fields. The count, summary, and route identifier fields must exist in the same geodatabase as the LRS Network. The Boundary Features and Summary Field parameters are only valid when no summary field is provided in the .json file. The output will be created based on the intersection of selected features from the LRS Network, summary layer(s), and feature count layer(s).", "section": "Usage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-lrs-data-product.html#usage

- (可選擇)
- 指定用于輸出長度欄位的測量單位。該參數只有在創建一個長度資料產品時才可用。 INCHES - 單位為英寸。 FEET - 單位為英尺。 YARDS - 單位為英尺。 YARDS - 單位為碼。 單位將為英里。 單位為海里。 INTMILES - 單位為法定英里。 INTFEET - 單位為國際英尺。 單位為毫米。 單位為米。 CENTIMETEERS - 單位為厘米。 METERS - 單位為米。 單位為米。 KILIMETERS - 單位為公里。 DECIMEERS - 單位為公里。- 單位將會是十進制。

NAUTICAL_MILES—The units will be nautical miles. INTMILES—The units will be statute miles. INTFEET—The units will be international feet. MILLIMETERS—The units will be millimeters. ## 生成 LRS 資料產品(遠程參考工具) · 代碼樣本

**id**　eca4a35bcd33703f44-27

**english**　Generate LRS Data Product

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 資料產品工具

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-lrs-data-product.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-lrs-data-product.html

**text**　# Name: GenerateLrsDataProduct_ex1.py
# Description: Transforms LRS data to create a data product for the selected routes in an LRS Network in a stand-alone script.
# Requirements: ArcGIS Linear Referencing

# Import arcpy module
import arcpy

# Local tool variables
in_template = r"C:\Data\Template.json"
in_route_features = r"C:\Data\SampleData.gdb\LRS\Network"
effective_date = "12/31/2024"
units = "METERS"
boundary_features = None
summary_field = None
exclude_null_summary_rows = "EXCLUDE"
output_format = "CSV"
out_file = r"C:\Data\LP1.csv"
out_table = None

# Run the tool
arcpy.lr.GenerateLrsDataProduct(in_template, in_route_features, effective_date, units, boundary_features, summary_field, exclude_null_summary_rows, output_format, out_file, out_table)

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "# Name: GenerateLrsDataProduct_ex1.py\n# Description: Transforms LRS data to create a data product for the selected routes in an LRS Network in a stand-alone script.\n# Requirements: ArcGIS Linear Referencing\n\n# Import arcpy module\nimport arcpy\n\n# Local tool variables\nin_template = r\"C:\\Data\\Template.json\"\nin_route_features = r\"C:\\Data\\SampleData.gdb\\LRS\\Network\"\neffective_date = \"12/31/2024\"\nunits = \"METERS\"\nboundary_features = None\nsummary_field = None\nexclude_null_summary_rows = \"EXCLUDE\"\noutput_format = \"CSV\"\nout_file = r\"C:\\Data\\LP1.csv\"\nout_table = None\n\n# Run the tool\narcpy.lr.GenerateLrsDataProduct(in_template, in_route_features, effective_date, units, boundary_features, summary_field, exclude_null_summary_rows, output_format, out_file, out_table)", "section": "Code sample"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-lrs-data-product.html#parameters

## 生成 LRS 資料產品(遠程參考工具) · 代碼樣本

**id**　eca4a35bcd33703f44-31

**english**　Generate LRS Data Product

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 資料產品工具

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-lrs-data-product.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-lrs-data-product.html

**text**　# Name: GenerateLrsDataProduct_ex3.py
# Description: Transforms LRS data to create a data product for the selected routes using a feature service.
# Requires: ArcGIS Linear Referencing

# Import arcpy module.
import arcpy

# Input event and target LRS network are in feature service.  Signing in portal is required to access the feature service.
arcpy.SignInToPortal('https://yourdomain.com/portal', 'username', 'password')

# Map the LRS network from the feature service. Here, 1 corresponds to the target LRS Network's layer ID.
in_route_features = r"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/1"

# Set tool variables
in_template = r"C:\Data\Template.json"
effective_date = "12/31/2024"
units = "FEET"
boundary_features = None
summary_field = None
exclude_null_summary_rows = "EXCLUDE"
output_format = "CSV"
out_file = r"C:\Data\LP3.csv"
out_table = None

# Run the tool
arcpy.lr.GenerateLrsDataProduct(in_template, in_route_features, effective_date, units, boundary_features, summary_field, exclude_null_summary_rows, output_format, out_file, out_table)

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "# Name: GenerateLrsDataProduct_ex3.py\n# Description: Transforms LRS data to create a data product for the selected routes using a feature service.\n# Requires: ArcGIS Linear Referencing\n\n# Import arcpy module.\nimport arcpy\n\n# Input event and target LRS network are in feature service.  Signing in portal is required to access the feature service.\narcpy.SignInToPortal('https://yourdomain.com/portal', 'username', 'password')\n\n# Map the LRS network from the feature service. Here, 1 corresponds to the target LRS Network's layer ID.\nin_route_features = r\"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/1\"\n\n# Set tool variables\nin_template = r\"C:\\Data\\Template.json\"\neffective_date = \"12/31/2024\"\nunits = \"FEET\"\nboundary_features = None\nsummary_field = None\nexclude_null_summary_rows = \"EXCLUDE\"\noutput_format = \"CSV\"\nout_file = r\"C:\\Data\\LP3.csv\"\nout_table = None\n\n# Run the tool\narcpy.lr.GenerateLrsDataProduct(in_template, in_route_features, effective_date, units, boundary_features, summary_field, exclude_null_summary_rows, output_format, out_file, out_table)", "section": "Code sample"}

## 生成 LRS 資料產品(遠程參考工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 資料產品工具

**english**　Generate LRS Data Product

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-lrs-data-product.html

**id**　eca4a35bcd33703f44-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-lrs-data-product.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Generate LRS Data Product，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。模板模板模板 [in_template]、輸入線路特征 [in_route_features]、生效日期 [effective_date]、長度單位(備選) [units]、邊界特征(可選) [boundary_features]、(備選) [summary_field]、排除空摘要行( 可選) [exclude_null_summary_rows]、輸出格式( 可選) [output_format]、輸出文件( 可選) [out_file]、產出表(備選) [out_table]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

## Remove Small Lines (Topographic Production Tools) · 注意：

**id**　eca7118a17fdcb9afe-3

**english**　Remove Small Lines

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 概化工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html

**text**　A warning is raised if the input features are not in a projected coordinate system. This tool relies on linear distance units, which will create unexpected results in an unprojected coordinate system. It is recommended that you run this tool on data in a projected coordinate system to ensure valid results. An error is raised and the tool will not process if the coordinate system is missing or unknown.
This tool identifies small lines as line segments that are shorter than the Minimum Length parameter value and do not intersect or connect to other features at one end. The connecting or intersecting features can be in the same input line feature class or in any of the intersecting features' feature classes. In most scenarios, the tool only uses the length of an individual feature when determining the minimum length. In some scenarios, the tool only uses the length of a segment of the line or the length of multiple lines.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html#usage

## Remove Small Lines (Topographic Production Tools) · 注意：

**id**　eca7118a17fdcb9afe-4

**english**　Remove Small Lines

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 概化工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html

**text**　When the Recursive parameter is checked, all small lines that exist in the data, including small lines created as a result of removing the previous small lines, will be removed. This can result in the removal of a significant number of features in areas that primarily contain short line segments.
Overshoots exist if the start or end of a line extends a short distance past the intersection of another feature in the same feature class but the line is not split at the intersection. Overshoots that are shorter than the Minimum Length parameter value are treated as small lines, even if the entire length of the line is longer than the Minimum Length value. When overshoots are identified, the feature will be modified to remove the overshoot. When the tool runs, a message will be returned indicating the number of features that were modified.
Providing the Maximum Angle parameter value may help when trying to keep small lines if multiple small lines connect to another feature and create a Y or T intersection. The angle for each small line is calculated as the declination of the small line from the continued path of the main line feature. For example, a T intersection in which the small line is perpendicular to the main road has a declination of 90 degrees, but a small line that continues in a straight line from the main road has a declination of 0.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html#usage

## Remove Small Lines (Topographic Production Tools) · 注意：

**id**　eca7118a17fdcb9afe-5

**english**　Remove Small Lines

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 概化工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html

**text**　When the Maximum Angle parameter value is provided, any small line with a declination angle larger than the Maximum Angle value will be removed. If multiple small lines have a declination that is smaller than the Maximum Angle value, the small line with the smallest declination may be kept.
When the Maximum Angle parameter value is provided , the final operation after any recursion is to determine if any of the small lines that were kept because they fall within the Maximum Angle value will be removed. In this case, the tool uses the overall length of the small line and connecting line features, not the length of the individual small line. If the length of the small line and any features it connects to, up to the first intersection, is shorter than the Minimum Length parameter value, the small line and connecting features will be removed. If the length of the combined features is longer than the Minimum Length value, the small line will be kept even though the individual feature is shorter than the Minimum Length value. This is because the geometries of small lines and connected features visually combine to create the appearance of a single segment that is longer than the Minimum Length value. You can run the Unsplit Line tool after the Remove Small Lines tool to combine the features into a single geometry that is longer than the Minimum Length value.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html#usage

## Remove Small Lines (Topographic Production Tools) · 注意：

**id**　eca7118a17fdcb9afe-6

**english**　Remove Small Lines

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 概化工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html

**text**　Features connected to any feature class provided for the Intersecting Features parameter are not considered small lines.
The integrity of the results of this tool relies on the topological integrity of the inputs. The following describe input data requirements and best practices: Connectivity—Proper connections must exist at intersections that truly represent the connectivity of the network. Intersecting features—Split lines at all true intersections, but not at overpasses and underpasses. Intersections that are not split in the appropriate place may produce unexpected results because the connectivity of the streets was not accurately assessed. False dead ends—A false dead end is an unconnected segment that appears visually connected when symbolized at the final map scale. These may be areas where you expect connectivity based on visual appearance, but features are not actually connected. If you run the tool without repairing the connectivity, unexpected disconnects may be identified as small lines and removed. Empty or null geometry—The Input Features parameter value must consist of valid geometries. If necessary, use the Repair Geometry tool to repair the features. Single-part features—Do not include multipart features in the Input Features parameter value. Use the Multipart To Singlepart tool or create a topology with a Must Be Single Part line rule to convert features to single-part features.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html#usage

## Remove Small Lines (Topographic Production Tools) · 注意：

**id**　eca7118a17fdcb9afe-7

**english**　Remove Small Lines

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 概化工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html

**text**　When the Split Input Lines parameter is checked, the tool will automatically split lines at intersections to ensure connectivity before determining if any small lines will be removed. If the tool determines a split feature will be removed, the original feature will be modified to remove only this portion of the line. If you know that the topological integrity of the inputs is correct, ensure that this parameter is unchecked (split_input_lines = "NO_SPLIT" in Python) for faster processing.
During processing, the Some geometries are not split at intersections warning message may appear. Check the Split Input Lines parameter or run a tool such as Feature To Line to ensure the appropriate segmentation before running the tool.
During processing, a warning appears when one or more small lines are maintained because they have a declination that is smaller than the Maximum Angle parameter value, and some features shorter than the minimum length may remain in the resulting data. While the length of the individual features may be shorter than the minimum length, the overall visual length of the small line and connecting line features will be longer than the minimum length.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html#usage

- 概化工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html

**parameter**　recursive

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- Recursive （可選）
- Specifies whether small lines on the line features will be removed. 選中—The small lines will be removed from the line features and the remaining lines will be analyzed to determine whether they are small lines. If they do not meet the Minimum Length parameter value, they will be identified as small lines and removed. 未選中—All the small lines on line features will be removed. The remaining lines will not be analyzed to determine whether they are small lines. This is the default.
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html#parameters

## Remove Small Lines (Topographic Production Tools) · 參數

**id**　eca7118a17fdcb9afe-14

**english**　Remove Small Lines

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 概化工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html

**parameter**　split_input_lines

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- Split Input Lines （可選）
- Specifies whether the input line features will be split at all intersections before determining the small lines to remove. 選中—All lines in the input feature class will be split at intersections to ensure topological integrity. The length of the split features, not the original feature geometries, will be used when applying the Minimum Length parameter value to determine small lines. This is the default. 未選中—The lines will not be split before determining the lines to remove. The length of the input feature geometries will be used when applying the Minimum Length parameter value to determine small lines.
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html#parameters

## Remove Small Lines (Topographic Production Tools) · 代碼示例

**id**　eca7118a17fdcb9afe-25

**english**　Remove Small Lines

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 概化工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html

**text**　# Name: RemoveSmallLines_sample.py
# Description: Remove Small Lines removes unconnected lines less than
# a certain length unless within a defined angle.

# Import System Modules
import arcpy

# Set workspace
arcpy.env.workspace = r'C:\data\LocalGovernment.gdb'

# Setting Local Variables
in_features = r'ReferenceData\RoadCenterline'
in_intersecting_features = r'ReferenceData\MedicalFacility'
in_featuresLyr = 'RoadL_Lyr'
in_intersecting_featuresLyr = 'MedicalFacility_Lyr'
minimum_length = '100 Feet'
maximum_angle = '45'
recursive = 'NON_RECURSIVE'

# Create an input feature layer for Remove Small Lines
arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(in_features,in_featuresLyr)
arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(in_intersecting_features, in_intersecting_featuresLyr)

# Run Remove Small Lines
arcpy.topographic.RemoveSmallLines(in_featuresLyr,minimum_length,maximum_angle,in_intersecting_featuresLyr,recursive)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html#derived-output

## Remove Small Lines (Topographic Production Tools) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱

**english**　Remove Small Lines

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html

**id**　eca7118a17fdcb9afe-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Remove Small Lines，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。Input Features [in_features]、Minimum Length [minimum_length]、Maximum Angle （可選） [maximum_angle]、Intersecting Features （可選） [in_intersecting_features]、Recursive （可選） [recursive]、Split Input Lines （可選） [split_input_lines]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/remove-small-lines.html#parameters

## 240025: 一個或多個 LineVariantElements 圖徵在 LVEShapeID 欄位中包含空值或無效值。 這些圖徵將使用 LineVariantElements 幾何，而非 LVEShapes 幾何。 · 描述

**id**　ecaef81c4db201eb1b-1

## 使用圖層和表視圖 · 使用圖層和表視圖

**id**　ecc06192ba18ec590f-1

**english**　Working with layers and table views

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 圖層和表視圖工具集
- 圖層和表視圖工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/working-with-layers-and-table-views.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/working-with-layers-and-table-views.html

**text**　工具的最常見輸入是資料集。當然，可以始終瀏覽至磁盤上的資料集，但是也可以使用圖層或表視圖作為輸入。
在 內容 窗格中看到的是圖層。圖層包含資料集路徑，以及符號信息和圖徵選擇信息。
表視圖是相當于圖層的表。它們是存儲在內存中的表，與將表添加至地圖時創建的表視圖相同。將在 獨立表 下的 內容 窗格中列出該表。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/working-with-layers-and-table-views.html

## 使用圖層和表視圖 · 注:

**id**　ecc06192ba18ec590f-2

**english**　Working with layers and table views

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 圖層和表視圖工具集
- 圖層和表視圖工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/working-with-layers-and-table-views.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/working-with-layers-and-table-views.html

**text**　在表上操作的任何工具也可以在圖徵類或圖徵圖層上操作。這是因為圖徵類只是具有包含圖徵形狀屬性的表。除此之外，它們只是表。
在 ArcGIS Pro 中打開地理處理工具或者在 Python 窗口中輸入工具名稱時，會發生以下情況：
將對工具參數進行檢查，以查看哪些參數使用輸入資料集或表。如果找到一個參數，則將進一步檢查輸入的類型，以確定其是否為點圖徵類、TIN、網絡、表或其他內容。
然后系統會掃描和過濾 內容 窗格，以確定是否有圖層與參數的資料集類型相匹配。如果是這樣，則系統會構建匹配圖層的列表，并將其作為參數的下拉列表提供。可以從這些圖層或表視圖中選擇一個作為輸入。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/working-with-layers-and-table-views.html

## 使用圖層和表視圖 · 使用圖層文件

**id**　ecc06192ba18ec590f-3

**english**　Working with layers and table views

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 圖層和表視圖工具集
- 圖層和表視圖工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/working-with-layers-and-table-views.html#080

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/working-with-layers-and-table-views.html

**text**　圖層可以圖層文件（擴展名為 .lyrx 的文件）的形式保存到磁盤，并用作接受圖層的任意工具的輸入。
圖層文件與圖層（例如 內容 窗格中的圖層）包含的信息相同，包括選擇圖徵的信息。
要創建一個圖層文件，可以使用 保存至圖層文件 工具。在 ArcGIS Pro 中，可以右鍵單擊 內容 窗格中的圖層，然后單擊 另存為圖層文件 。
對于接受圖層的任意工具，可以瀏覽至磁盤上的圖層文件 (.lyrx)，就象瀏覽至磁盤上的資料集一樣。但是，瀏覽至圖層文件時，有些工具不能確定圖層文件中包含的資料類型，因此會顯示全部的圖層文件。如果選擇的圖層文件不當，工具會顯示錯誤或警告。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/working-with-layers-and-table-views.html#080

## 多維網格相關性 (影像分析工具) · 匯總

**id**　ecc72ce3fb469917e5-1

**english**　Multidimensional Raster Correlation

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 多維分析工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/multidimensional-raster-correlation.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/multidimensional-raster-correlation.html

**text**　分析一個或兩個多維網格中兩個變量之間的相關性。
該工具以兩個多維網格作為輸入，使用 Pearson、Kendall 或 Spearman 相關性方法比較兩個變量，并輸出一個相關性網格，每個像素代表相應像素數組的相關性值。 輸出的網格可以映射兩個變量相關和不相關的區域。
工具還可以在滯后值非零時計算互相關性，并在兩個輸入相同時計算自相關性。
您可以分析一個或兩個多維網格中兩個變量之間的相關性。 輸出是一個相關性網格，其中每個像素代表兩個變量的兩個時間序列的相關性。 該工具可用于計算滯后的相關性、互相關性或自相關性。 例如，下面圖像中的相關性網格是根據隨時間變化的土壤水分變量和降水變量計算得出的。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/multidimensional-raster-correlation.html#summary

## 多維網格相關性 (影像分析工具) · 用法

**id**　ecc72ce3fb469917e5-2

**english**　Multidimensional Raster Correlation

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 多維分析工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/multidimensional-raster-correlation.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/multidimensional-raster-correlation.html

**text**　對于兩個多維網格中的每個像素位置，工具使用兩個變量的兩個相應像素數組并計算一個相關性值。 工具比較每個重疊像素的兩個有序數組，修剪不必要的資料。 當像素數組的長度不同時，將修剪較長數組的尾部。
兩個多維網格必須具有相同的像元大小、空間范圍和投影。 它們不需要相同的時間范圍或間隔。
如果需要相同的間隔，請使用聚合多維網格工具或按維度插值網格函數來重新采樣維度間隔。
使用子集化多維網格工具或多維過濾器函數來定義相同的維度范圍。
或者，您可以使用延遲參數來控制如何在維度上對齊兩個變量以通過指定的步長移動像素數組來計算相關性值。 值為 0 表示無平移，值為 2 表示將第一個變量向前平移兩個索引（步長）來計算，值為 -2 表示將第一個變量向后平移兩個步長來計算。 例如，第一個網格中的像素數組是 0、1、2、3，第二個網格中的像素數組是 4、5、6、7、8、9。 使用延遲參數，在維度上對齊兩個變量。 對于值 0：將計算 0、1、2、3 與 4、5、6、7；對于值 2：將計算 0、1、2、3 與 6、7、8、9；對于值 -1：將計算 1、2、3 與 4、5、6。
延遲描述了一個（領先）變量與另一個（滯后）變量稍后時間的值互相關和同步的情況。
互相關性計算指定范圍內每個延遲的相關性網格，生成的相關性柵將被保存為多波段網格。 當選中計算 P 值參數時，將在輸出中添加附加的波段。 設計工作流以避免創建不必要的輸出波段。
要計算自相關性，需將第一個和第二個輸入設置為相同的多維網格、維度名稱和變量。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/multidimensional-raster-correlation.html#usage

- 指定將使用的相關性計算方法。 Pearson—相關性方法將為 Pearson。 這是默認設置。 Spearman—相關性方法將為 Spearman。 Kendall—相關性方法將為 Kendall。
- String

- 標注
- 說明

- 延遲 （可選）
- 通過指定的步長平移像素數組來計算相關性值，從 0 到維度/2，取決于時差。 默認值為 0。

## 多維網格相關性 (影像分析工具) · 參數

**id**　ecc72ce3fb469917e5-12

**english**　Multidimensional Raster Correlation

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 多維分析工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/multidimensional-raster-correlation.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/multidimensional-raster-correlation.html

**parameter**　calculate_xcorr

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 計算滯后時的互相關性 （可選）
- 指定是否計算滯后時的互相關性。 啟用時，將在滯后值定義的范圍內的每個滯后處計算相關性。 例如，如果滯后值為 2，則將計算 -2、-1、0、1 和 2 的相關性，并將其存儲為輸出網格中的波段。 選中—將計算滯后時的互相關性。 未選中—不計算滯后時的互相關性。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/multidimensional-raster-correlation.html#parameters

**id**　ecc72ce3fb469917e5-13

**english**　Multidimensional Raster Correlation

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱

**parameter**　calculate_pvalue

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 計算 P 值 （可選）
- 指定是否計算滯后時的 p 值。 P 值是描述兩個變量相關程度的置信值。 選中—將計算滯后時的 p 值。 輸出將包括存儲 p 值的其他波段。 未選中—不計算滯后時的 p 值。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/multidimensional-raster-correlation.html#parameters

- 一個包含最大相關性值及其對應滯后值的雙波段網格。 選中計算滯后時的互相關性參數時，將創建此網格。

## 多維網格相關性 (影像分析工具) · 返回值

**id**　ecc72ce3fb469917e5-18

**english**　Multidimensional Raster Correlation

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 多維分析工具集

**parameter**　in_mdim_raster2

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- in_mdim_raster2
- 將與第一個輸入相關聯的第二個多維網格。 計算中使用的維度長度必須大于 2。 如果 in_mdim_raster1 參數值與 in_mdim_raster2 參數值相同，則將計算自相關性。 自相關性指的是兩個連續時間間隔之間相同變量的相關程度。
- Raster Dataset; Raster Layer; Mosaic Dataset; Mosaic Layer; Image Service
